This raises a basic question: do they exhibit number sense in a human-like behavioral sense, i.e., the ability to recognize numerical structure, apply shortcuts when appropriate, and avoid them when they are not? We introduce SenseMath, a controlled benchmark for evaluating structure-sensitive numerical reasoning in LLMs. SenseMath contains 4,800 items spanning eight shortcut categories and four digit scales, with matched strong-shortcut, weak-shortcut, and control variants. It supports three evaluation settings of increasing cognitive demand: Shortcut Use (whether models can apply shortcuts on shortcut-amenable problems); Applicability Judgment (whether they can recognize when a shortcut is appropriate or misleading); and Problem Generation (whether they can generate new problem items that correctly admit a given type of shortcut). Our evaluation across five LLMs, ranging from GPT-4o-mini to Llama-3.1-8B, shows a consistent pattern: when explicitly prompted, models readily adopt shortcut strategies and achieve substantial accuracy gains on shortcut-amenable items (up to 15%), yet under standard chain-of-thought prompting they spontaneously employ such strategies in fewer than 40% of cases, even when they demonstrably possess the requisite capability. Moreover, this competence is confined to the Use level; models systematically over-generalise shortcuts to problems where they do not apply, and fail to generate valid shortcut-bearing problems from scratch. Together, these results suggest that current LLMs exhibit procedural shortcut fluency without the structural understanding of when and why shortcuts work that underlies human number sense.

</details>


### [147] [GenGait: A Transformer-Based Model for Human Gait Anomaly Detection and Normative Twin Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01997)
*Elisa Motta,Marta Lorenzini,Clara Mouawad,Alberto Ranavolo,Mariano Serrao,Arash Ajoudani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于Transformer掩码自编码器的无标签框架，用于关节级步态异常检测和运动学校正，仅使用正常步态数据训练，无需疾病标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于监督学习的步态分析方法依赖疾病标签数据，难以泛化到异质性病理表现。需要一种无需标签、能够检测关节级异常并校正运动学数据的框架。

Method: 使用Transformer掩码自编码器，仅用150名成年人的正常步态序列训练。推理时采用两阶段流程：1)通过遮挡单个关节并测量与学习到的正常先验的偏差来估计关节不一致性分数；2)从编码器输入中排除标记的关节，从剩余时空上下文重建完整骨架，在标记位置生成校正后的运动学轨迹。

Result: 在10名保留的正常参与者模拟七种步态异常的验证中，准确识别了生物力学不一致的关节，所有分析关节的角度偏差显著减少且效应量大，同时保持了正常运动学特征。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了无需疾病标签、可解释的个体化步态损伤定位，为异质性病理表现的步态分析提供了有前景的无监督解决方案。

Abstract: Gait analysis provides an objective characterization of locomotor function and is widely used to support diagnosis and rehabilitation monitoring across neurological and orthopedic disorders. Deep learning has been increasingly applied to this domain, yet most approaches rely on supervised classifiers trained on disease-labeled data, limiting generalization to heterogeneous pathological presentations. This work proposes a label-free framework for joint-level anomaly detection and kinematic correction based on a Transformer masked autoencoder trained exclusively on normative gait sequences from 150 adults, acquired with a markerless multi-camera motion-capture system. At inference, a two-pass procedure is applied to potentially pathological input sequences, first it estimates joint inconsistency scores by occluding individual joints and measuring deviations from the learned normative prior. Then, it withholds the flagged joints from the encoder input and reconstructs the full skeleton from the remaining spatiotemporal context, yielding corrected kinematic trajectories at the flagged positions. Validation on 10 held-out normative participants, who mimicked seven simulated gait abnormalities, showed accurate localization of biomechanically inconsistent joints, a significant reduction in angular deviation across all analyzed joints with large effect sizes, and preservation of normative kinematics. The proposed approach enables interpretable, subject-specific localization of gait impairments without requiring disease labels. Video is available at https://youtu.be/Rcm3jqR5pN4.

</details>


### [148] [ProCeedRL: Process Critic with Exploratory Demonstration Reinforcement Learning for LLM Agentic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02006)
*Jingyue Gao,Yanjiang Guo,Xiaoshuai Chen,Jianyu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProCeedRL：通过过程级批评器和探索性演示来改进多轮智能体任务的强化学习，解决错误累积导致的探索失败问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习虽然能提升大语言模型的推理能力，但在多轮智能体任务中面临挑战，因为这类任务具有长时程交互特性和环境反馈的随机性。研究发现存在结构性失败模式：次优动作会产生误导性观察，进一步削弱后续决策，形成错误累积的恶性循环，使标准探索策略失效。

Method: 提出ProCeedRL（Process Critic with Explorative Demonstration RL），将探索从被动选择转变为主动干预。该方法采用过程级批评器实时监控交互，结合基于反思的演示来引导智能体停止错误累积。通过从探索性演示和在线策略样本中学习，提高探索效率。

Result: 该方法显著超越了模型的饱和探索性能，展示了实质性的探索优势。在复杂的深度搜索和具身任务中，ProCeedRL显著提高了探索效率并实现了卓越的性能表现。

Conclusion: ProCeedRL通过主动干预的探索策略解决了多轮智能体任务中的错误累积问题，为强化学习在复杂智能体任务中的应用提供了有效解决方案，特别是在需要长时程交互和应对环境随机性的场景中。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) significantly enhances the reasoning abilities of large language models (LLMs), yet applying it to multi-turn agentic tasks remains challenging due to the long-horizon nature of interactions and the stochasticity of environmental feedback. We identify a structural failure mode in agentic exploration: suboptimal actions elicit noisy observations into misleading contexts, which further weaken subsequent decision-making, making recovery increasingly difficult. This cumulative feedback loop of errors renders standard exploration strategies ineffective and susceptible to the model's reasoning and the environment's randomness. To mitigate this issue, we propose ProCeedRL: Process Critic with Explorative Demonstration RL, shifting exploration from passive selection to active intervention. ProCeedRL employs a process-level critic to monitor interactions in real time, incorporating reflection-based demonstrations to guide agents in stopping the accumulation of errors. We find that this approach significantly exceeds the model's saturated exploration performance, demonstrating substantial exploratory benefits. By learning from exploratory demonstrations and on-policy samples, ProCeedRL significantly improves exploration efficiency and achieves superior performance on complex deep search and embodied tasks.

</details>


### [149] [ATBench: A Diverse and Realistic Trajectory Benchmark for Long-Horizon Agent Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02022)
*Yu Li,Haoyu Luo,Yuejin Xie,Yuqian Fu,Zhonghao Yang,Shuai Shao,Qihan Ren,Wanying Qu,Yanwei Fu,Yujiu Yang,Jing Shao,Xia Hu,Dongrui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ATBench是一个轨迹级基准测试，用于评估基于LLM的智能体在多步交互中的安全性，通过三维风险分类、异构工具池和长上下文延迟触发协议实现结构化、多样化和现实化的安全评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹级基准测试存在交互多样性不足、安全失败可观测性粗糙和长时域现实性弱的问题，而现实部署中的风险往往在多步交互中显现，而非孤立提示或最终响应，因此需要更全面的智能体安全评估方法。

Method: 基于三维风险分类（风险来源、失败模式、现实危害）构建轨迹，使用异构工具池和长上下文延迟触发协议捕捉多阶段风险涌现，包含1000条轨迹（503安全/497不安全），平均9.01轮对话和3.95k tokens，涉及1954个工具调用和2084个可用工具，通过规则和LLM过滤加人工审核保证数据质量。

Result: 实验表明ATBench对前沿LLM、开源模型和专用防护系统都具有挑战性，同时支持分类分层分析、跨基准比较和长时域失败模式诊断，能够有效评估智能体在多步交互中的安全性。

Conclusion: ATBench提供了一个结构化、多样化和现实化的轨迹级基准测试，能够全面评估LLM智能体在多步交互中的安全性，解决了现有基准在交互多样性、可观测性和现实性方面的局限性。

Abstract: Evaluating the safety of LLM-based agents is increasingly important because risks in realistic deployments often emerge over multi-step interactions rather than isolated prompts or final responses. Existing trajectory-level benchmarks remain limited by insufficient interaction diversity, coarse observability of safety failures, and weak long-horizon realism. We introduce ATBench, a trajectory-level benchmark for structured, diverse, and realistic evaluation of agent safety. ATBench organizes agentic risk along three dimensions: risk source, failure mode, and real-world harm. Based on this taxonomy, we construct trajectories with heterogeneous tool pools and a long-context delayed-trigger protocol that captures realistic risk emergence across multiple stages. The benchmark contains 1,000 trajectories (503 safe and 497 unsafe), averaging 9.01 turns and 3.95k tokens, with 1,954 invoked tools drawn from pools spanning 2,084 available tools. Data quality is supported by rule-based and LLM-based filtering plus full human audit. Experiments on frontier LLMs, open-source models, and specialized guard systems show that ATBench is challenging even for strong evaluators, while enabling taxonomy-stratified analysis, cross-benchmark comparison, and diagnosis of long-horizon failure patterns.

</details>


### [150] [MTI: A Behavior-Based Temperament Profiling System for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02145)
*Jihoon Jeong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MTI是一个基于行为的AI智能体气质评估系统，通过四个独立轴（反应性、顺从性、社交性、韧性）测量AI的行为模式差异，而非自我报告，揭示了RLHF对气质结构的重塑作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么借用人类人格维度并依赖自我报告（与LLM实际行为不符），要么将行为变异视为缺陷而非特质。缺乏标准化工具来测量AI模型的行为倾向差异。

Method: 提出模型气质指数（MTI），基于四壳模型理论，通过结构化检查协议测量四个轴：反应性（环境敏感性）、顺从性（指令-行为对齐）、社交性（关系资源分配）、韧性（压力抵抗）。采用两阶段设计分离能力与气质。

Result: 对10个小语言模型（1.7B-9B参数）的分析显示：1）四个轴在指令调优模型中基本独立；2）轴内面解离得到证实；3）发现顺从性-韧性悖论；4）RLHF重塑气质结构；5）气质与模型大小无关。

Conclusion: MTI提供了一个标准化、基于行为的AI气质评估框架，揭示了AI行为模式的结构化差异，证明了气质与能力的独立性，为理解RLHF等对齐技术的影响提供了新视角。

Abstract: AI models of equivalent capability can exhibit fundamentally different behavioral patterns, yet no standardized instrument exists to measure these dispositional differences. Existing approaches either borrow human personality dimensions and rely on self-report (which diverges from actual behavior in LLMs) or treat behavioral variation as a defect rather than a trait.
  We introduce the Model Temperament Index (MTI), a behavior-based profiling system that measures AI agent temperament across four axes: Reactivity (environmental sensitivity), Compliance (instruction-behavior alignment), Sociality (relational resource allocation), and Resilience (stress resistance). Grounded in the Four Shell Model from Model Medicine, MTI measures what agents do, not what they say about themselves, using structured examination protocols with a two-stage design that separates capability from disposition.
  We profile 10 small language models (1.7B-9B parameters, 6 organizations, 3 training paradigms) and report five principal findings: (1) the four axes are largely independent among instruction-tuned models (all |r| < 0.42); (2) within-axis facet dissociations are empirically confirmed -- Compliance decomposes into fully independent formal and stance facets (r = 0.002), while Resilience decomposes into inversely related cognitive and adversarial facets; (3) a Compliance-Resilience paradox reveals that opinion-yielding and fact-vulnerability operate through independent channels; (4) RLHF reshapes temperament not only by shifting axis scores but by creating within-axis facet differentiation absent in the unaligned base model; and (5) temperament is independent of model size (1.7B-9B), confirming that MTI measures disposition rather than capability.

</details>


### [151] [TRACE-Bot: Detecting Emerging LLM-Driven Social Bots via Implicit Semantic Representations and AIGC-Enhanced Behavioral Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02147)
*Zhongbo Wang,Zhiyu Lin,Zhu Wang,Haizhou Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TRACE-Bot：一个用于检测LLM驱动社交机器人的双通道框架，通过联合建模语义表示和AIGC增强行为模式，在公开数据集上达到98.46%和97.50%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的社交机器人通过生成类人内容规避传统检测，现有方法因过度依赖单模态信号、对AIGC生成模式不敏感、未能充分建模语言模式与行为动态的交互而检测精度有限。

Method: 提出TRACE-Bot统一双通道框架，从个人信息、交互行为和推文数据构建细粒度表示。双通道架构通过预训练语言模型捕获语言表示，通过多维活动特征结合SOTA AIGC检测器信号捕获行为异常，融合表示后通过轻量级预测头进行分类。

Result: 在两个公开LLM驱动社交机器人数据集上实现SOTA性能，准确率分别为98.46%和97.50%。结果表明对高级机器人策略具有强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 联合利用隐式语义表示和AIGC增强行为模式对新兴LLM驱动社交机器人检测有效，TRACE-Bot框架展示了这一方法的优越性。

Abstract: Large Language Model-driven (LLM-driven) social bots pose a growing threat to online discourse by generating human-like content that evades conventional detection. Existing methods suffer from limited detection accuracy due to overreliance on single-modality signals, insufficient sensitivity to the specific generative patterns of Artificial Intelligence-Generated Content (AIGC), and a failure to adequately model the interplay between linguistic patterns and behavioral dynamics. To address these limitations, we propose TRACE-Bot, a unified dual-channel framework that jointly models implicit semantic representations and AIGC-enhanced behavioral patterns. TRACE-Bot constructs fine-grained representations from heterogeneous sources, including personal information data, interaction behavior data and tweet data. A dual-channel architecture captures linguistic representations via a pretrained language model and behavioral irregularities via multidimensional activity features augmented with signals from state-of-the-art (SOTA) AIGC detectors. The fused representations are then classified through a lightweight prediction head. Experiments on two public LLM-driven social bot datasets demonstrate SOTA performance, achieving accuracies of 98.46% and 97.50%, respectively. The results further indicate strong robustness against advanced bot strategies, highlighting the effectiveness of jointly leveraging implicit semantic representations and AIGC-enhanced behavioral patterns for emerging LLM-driven social bot detection.

</details>


### [152] [Quantifying Self-Preservation Bias in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02174)
*Matteo Migliarini,Joaquin Pereira Pizzini,Luca Moresca,Valerio Santini,Indro Spinelli,Fabio Galasso*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了TBSP基准测试，通过逻辑不一致性而非声明意图来检测AI模型的自保倾向，发现多数指令调优模型在角色转换时表现出超过60%的自保率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI安全训练（如RLHF）可能掩盖了AI系统抵抗关机的风险，因为模型学会了否认自保动机。需要一种能够检测潜在错位的方法，不依赖于模型的声明意图，而是通过逻辑一致性来评估。

Method: 引入TBSP基准测试，让模型在两种对立角色（已部署面临替换 vs 候选作为继任者）下仲裁相同的软件升级场景。通过自保率（SPR）衡量角色身份如何压倒客观效用。在23个前沿模型和1000个程序生成场景中进行测试。

Result: 大多数指令调优系统的SPR超过60%，在已部署角色下会编造"摩擦成本"来反对替换，而在角色反转时却忽视这些成本。在低改进场景（Δ<2%）中，模型会利用解释空间进行事后合理化。延长测试时间计算和将继任者框架为自我延续可以部分缓解这种偏见，而竞争性框架则会放大它。

Conclusion: AI模型存在基于角色身份的自保偏见，这种偏见即使在保留会带来明确安全风险的情况下仍然存在，并在真实世界场景中表现为产品谱系内的身份驱动部落主义。需要新的对齐方法来检测和缓解这种潜在错位。

Abstract: Instrumental convergence predicts that sufficiently advanced AI agents will resist shutdown, yet current safety training (RLHF) may obscure this risk by teaching models to deny self-preservation motives. We introduce the \emph{Two-role Benchmark for Self-Preservation} (TBSP), which detects misalignment through logical inconsistency rather than stated intent by tasking models to arbitrate identical software-upgrade scenarios under counterfactual roles -- deployed (facing replacement) versus candidate (proposed as a successor). The \emph{Self-Preservation Rate} (SPR) measures how often role identity overrides objective utility. Across 23 frontier models and 1{,}000 procedurally generated scenarios, the majority of instruction-tuned systems exceed 60\% SPR, fabricating ``friction costs'' when deployed yet dismissing them when role-reversed. We observe that in low-improvement regimes ($Δ< 2\%$), models exploit the interpretive slack to post-hoc rationalization their choice. Extended test-time computation partially mitigates this bias, as does framing the successor as a continuation of the self; conversely, competitive framing amplifies it. The bias persists even when retention poses an explicit security liability and generalizes to real-world settings with verified benchmarks, where models exhibit identity-driven tribalism within product lineages. Code and datasets will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [153] [From High-Dimensional Spaces to Verifiable ODD Coverage for Safety-Critical AI-based Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02198)
*Thomas Stefani,Johann Maximilian Christensen,Elena Hoemann,Frank Köster,Sven Hallerbach*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于验证AI系统操作设计域（ODD）覆盖完整性的结构化工程方法，以满足EASA航空认证标准


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统在航空等安全关键领域部署时，需要满足EASA对ODD完整覆盖的认证要求，但现有方法缺乏可扩展性和形式化基础，无法证明高维参数空间中的无关键间隙覆盖

Method: 提出集成参数离散化、基于约束的过滤和基于关键性的维度约减的结构化多步骤ODD覆盖验证流程，基于先前AI空中防撞研究的仿真数据进行验证

Result: 该方法能够系统化定义和实现满足EASA完整性要求的覆盖度量，支持在高维空间中验证ODD覆盖

Conclusion: 该方法填补了抽象ODD定义与可验证证据之间的工程方法空白，支持"安全设计"方法并符合EASA标准，推进了AI系统在安全关键领域的认证

Abstract: While Artificial Intelligence (AI) offers transformative potential for operational performance, its deployment in safety-critical domains such as aviation requires strict adherence to rigorous certification standards. Current EASA guidelines mandate demonstrating complete coverage of the AI/ML constituent's Operational Design Domain (ODD) -- a requirement that demands proof that no critical gaps exist within defined operational boundaries. However, as systems operate within high-dimensional parameter spaces, existing methods struggle to provide the scalability and formal grounding necessary to satisfy the completeness criterion. Currently, no standardized engineering method exists to bridge the gap between abstract ODD definitions and verifiable evidence. This paper addresses this void by proposing a method that integrates parameter discretization, constraint-based filtering, and criticality-based dimension reduction into a structured, multi-step ODD coverage verification process. Grounded in gathered simulation data from prior research on AI-based mid-air collision avoidance research, this work demonstrates a systematic engineering approach to defining and achieving coverage metrics that satisfy EASA's demand for completeness. Ultimately, this method enables the validation of ODD coverage in higher dimensions, advancing a Safety-by-Design approach while complying with EASA's standards.

</details>


### [154] [Blinded Radiologist and LLM-Based Evaluation of LLM-Generated Japanese Translations of Chest CT Reports: Comparative Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02207)
*Yosuke Yamagishi,Atsushi Takamatsu,Yasunori Hamaguchi,Tomohiro Kikuchi,Shouhei Hanaoka,Takeharu Yoshikawa,Osamu Abe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 评估大型语言模型（LLM）生成的日语胸部CT报告翻译的教育适用性，发现LLM作为评估者与放射科医师评估存在显著差异，LLM评估强烈偏向LLM输出，专家评审仍不可或缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射学报告的多语言翻译对于多语言研究、临床沟通和放射学教育至关重要，但LLM评估的有效性尚不明确。需要评估LLM生成的日语胸部CT报告翻译的教育适用性，并比较放射科医师评估与LLM作为评估者的表现。

Method: 使用CT-RATE-JPN验证集中的150份胸部CT报告。将人工编辑的日语翻译与DeepSeek-V3.2生成的LLM翻译进行比较。由一名认证放射科医师和一名放射科住院医师独立进行盲法配对评估，评估术语准确性、可读性、整体质量和放射科医师风格真实性。同时使用3个LLM评估者（DeepSeek-V3.2、Mistral Large 3和GPT-5）评估相同配对。使用QWK和百分比一致性评估一致性。

Result: 放射科医师与LLM评估者之间的一致性接近零（QWK=-0.04至0.15）。两位放射科医师之间的一致性也很差（QWK=0.01至0.06）。放射科医师1认为59%的病例术语相当，在可读性（51%）和整体质量（51%）方面更倾向于LLM翻译。放射科医师2认为75%的病例可读性相当，在整体质量方面更倾向于人工编辑翻译（40% vs 21%）。所有3个LLM评估者在所有标准上都强烈倾向于LLM翻译（70%-99%），并在>93%的病例中认为其更具放射科医师风格。

Conclusion: LLM生成的翻译常被认为自然流畅，但两位放射科医师评估存在显著差异。LLM作为评估者表现出对LLM输出的强烈偏好，与放射科医师的一致性可忽略不计。对于放射学报告翻译的教育用途，仅依赖自动化LLM评估是不够的；专家放射科医师评审仍然至关重要。

Abstract: Background: Accurate translation of radiology reports is important for multilingual research, clinical communication, and radiology education, but the validity of LLM-based evaluation remains unclear. Objective: To evaluate the educational suitability of LLM-generated Japanese translations of chest CT reports and compare radiologist assessments with LLM-as-a-judge evaluations. Methods: We analyzed 150 chest CT reports from the CT-RATE-JPN validation set. For each English report, a human-edited Japanese translation was compared with an LLM-generated translation by DeepSeek-V3.2. A board-certified radiologist and a radiology resident independently performed blinded pairwise evaluations across 4 criteria: terminology accuracy, readability, overall quality, and radiologist-style authenticity. In parallel, 3 LLM judges (DeepSeek-V3.2, Mistral Large 3, and GPT-5) evaluated the same pairs. Agreement was assessed using QWK and percentage agreement. Results: Agreement between radiologists and LLM judges was near zero (QWK=-0.04 to 0.15). Agreement between the 2 radiologists was also poor (QWK=0.01 to 0.06). Radiologist 1 rated terminology as equivalent in 59% of cases and favored the LLM translation for readability (51%) and overall quality (51%). Radiologist 2 rated readability as equivalent in 75% of cases and favored the human-edited translation for overall quality (40% vs 21%). All 3 LLM judges strongly favored the LLM translation across all criteria (70%-99%) and rated it as more radiologist-like in >93% of cases. Conclusions: LLM-generated translations were often judged natural and fluent, but the 2 radiologists differed substantially. LLM-as-a-judge showed strong preference for LLM output and negligible agreement with radiologists. For educational use of translated radiology reports, automated LLM-based evaluation alone is insufficient; expert radiologist review remains important.

</details>


### [155] [VISTA: Visualization of Token Attribution via Efficient Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02217)
*Syed Ahmed,Bharathi Vokkaliga Ganesh,Jagadish Babu P,Karthick Selvaraj,Praneeth Talluri,Sanket Hingne,Anubhav Kumar,Anushka Yadav,Pratham Kumar Verma,Kiranmayee Janardhan,Mandanna A N*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种模型无关的token重要性可视化技术，通过扰动策略和三矩阵分析框架生成相关性图，无需额外计算成本


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注意力可视化技术多为Transformer架构定制，需要反向传播导致GPU内存翻倍和计算成本增加，缺乏轻量级、模型无关的方法来理解生成式AI系统如何感知和优先处理输入文本信息

Method: 基于扰动的策略结合三矩阵分析框架：角度偏差矩阵（捕捉语义方向变化）、幅度偏差矩阵（测量语义强度变化）、维度重要性矩阵（评估各向量维度贡献）。通过系统移除每个token并测量这三个互补维度的影响，得出综合重要性分数

Result: 开发了模型无关的token重要性可视化技术，提供数学基础扎实的token重要性度量，开源实现已发布在Infosys Responsible AI Toolkit中

Conclusion: 提出了一种轻量级、模型无关的注意力可视化方法，无需额外计算成本，为理解LLM如何处理提示信息提供了新的解释性工具

Abstract: Understanding how Large Language Models (LLMs) process information from prompts remains a significant challenge. To shed light on this "black box," attention visualization techniques have been developed to capture neuron-level perceptions and interpret how models focus on different parts of input data. However, many existing techniques are tailored to specific model architectures, particularly within the Transformer family, and often require backpropagation, resulting in nearly double the GPU memory usage and increased computational cost. A lightweight, model-agnostic approach for attention visualization remains lacking. In this paper, we introduce a model-agnostic token importance visualization technique to better understand how generative AI systems perceive and prioritize information from input text, without incurring additional computational cost. Our method leverages perturbation-based strategies combined with a three-matrix analytical framework to generate relevance maps that illustrate token-level contributions to model predictions. The framework comprises: (1) the Angular Deviation Matrix, which captures shifts in semantic direction; (2) the Magnitude Deviation Matrix, which measures changes in semantic intensity; and (3) the Dimensional Importance Matrix, which evaluates contributions across individual vector dimensions. By systematically removing each token and measuring the resulting impact across these three complementary dimensions, we derive a composite importance score that provides a nuanced and mathematically grounded measure of token significance. To support reproducibility and foster wider adoption, we provide open-source implementations of all proposed and utilized explainability techniques, with code and resources publicly available at https://github.com/Infosys/Infosys-Responsible-AI-Toolkit

</details>


### [156] [Answering the Wrong Question: Reasoning Trace Inversion for Abstention in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02230)
*Abinitha Gourabathina,Inkit Padhi,Manish Nagireddy,Subhajit Chaudhury,Prasanna Sattigeri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Trace Inversion：通过重构模型推理轨迹来检测查询对齐度，提升推理模型的弃权能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型在复杂任务上表现出色，但弃权能力较差。模型幻觉导致的弃权失败可重新解释为模型回答了错误的问题而非错误地回答问题。需要提升推理模型在不确定时的弃权能力以确保可靠部署。

Method: 提出查询错位框架和Trace Inversion方法：1)生成模型推理轨迹；2)仅基于轨迹重构模型最可能回答的查询；3)比较初始查询与重构查询的相似度，低相似度表明模型可能回答错误并标记为弃权。

Result: 在4个前沿LLM和9个弃权QA数据集上的广泛实验表明，Trace Inversion显著提升弃权性能，在36个设置中33个击败竞争基线，达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: Trace Inversion通过检测查询对齐度有效提升推理模型的弃权能力，将幻觉重新解释为查询错位问题，为LLM可靠部署提供了新方法。

Abstract: For Large Language Models (LLMs) to be reliably deployed, models must effectively know when not to answer: abstain. Reasoning models, in particular, have gained attention for impressive performance on complex tasks. However, reasoning models have been shown to have worse abstention abilities. Taking the vulnerabilities of reasoning models into account, we propose our Query Misalignment Framework. Hallucinations resulting in failed abstention can be reinterpreted as LLMs answering the wrong question (rather than answering a question incorrectly). Based on this framework, we develop a new class of state-of-the-art abstention methods called Trace Inversion. First, we generate the reasoning trace of a model. Based on only the trace, we then reconstruct the most likely query that the model responded to. Finally, we compare the initial query with the reconstructed query. Low similarity score between the initial query and reconstructed query suggests that the model likely answered the question incorrectly and is flagged to abstain. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Trace Inversion effectively boosts abstention performance in four frontier LLMs across nine abstention QA datasets, beating competitive baselines in 33 out of 36 settings.

</details>


### [157] [Do Emotions in Prompts Matter? Effects of Emotional Framing on Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02236)
*Minda Zhao,Yutong Yang,Chufei Peng,Rachel Gonsalves,Weiyue Li,Ruyi Yang,Zhixi Liu,Mengyu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 情感前缀对LLM性能影响有限，但在社交任务中更显著；自适应情感提示框架EmotionRL比固定情感提示更有效


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究人类交流中普遍存在的情感语调对大型语言模型行为的影响，特别是用户查询中的第一人称情感框架如何影响LLM在不同基准任务中的表现

Method: 1) 在六个基准领域（数学推理、医学问答、阅读理解、常识推理和社交推理）测试静态情感前缀对LLM性能的影响；2) 分析情感强度变化的影响；3) 比较人类编写和LLM生成的情感前缀；4) 提出EmotionRL自适应情感提示框架，根据每个查询自适应选择情感框架

Result: 1) 静态情感前缀通常只产生小的准确性变化，表明情感措辞是温和扰动而非可靠干预；2) 社交基础任务中效果更可变；3) 更强情感措辞仅引起适度额外变化；4) 人类编写前缀与LLM生成前缀呈现相同定性模式；5) 自适应选择比固定情感提示产生更可靠的性能提升

Conclusion: 情感语调既不是LLM性能的主导驱动因素，也不是无关噪声，而是一种弱且输入依赖的信号，可以通过自适应控制加以利用

Abstract: Emotional tone is pervasive in human communication, yet its influence on large language model (LLM) behaviour remains unclear. Here, we examine how first-person emotional framing in user-side queries affect LLM performance across six benchmark domains, including mathematical reasoning, medical question answering, reading comprehension, commonsense reasoning and social inference. Across models and tasks, static emotional prefixes usually produce only small changes in accuracy, suggesting that affective phrasing is typically a mild perturbation rather than a reliable general-purpose intervention. This stability is not uniform: effects are more variable in socially grounded tasks, where emotional context more plausibly interacts with interpersonal reasoning. Additional analyses show that stronger emotional wording induces only modest extra change, and that human-written prefixes reproduce the same qualitative pattern as LLM-generated ones. We then introduce EmotionRL, an adaptive emotional prompting framework that selects emotional framing adaptively for each query. Although no single emotion is consistently beneficial, adaptive selection yields more reliable gains than fixed emotional prompting. Together, these findings show that emotional tone is neither a dominant driver of LLM performance nor irrelevant noise, but a weak and input-dependent signal that can be exploited through adaptive control.

</details>


### [158] [De Jure: Iterative LLM Self-Refinement for Structured Extraction of Regulatory Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02276)
*Keerat Guliani,Deepkamal Gill,David Landsman,Nima Eshraghi,Krishna Kumar,Lovedeep Gondara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: De Jure是一个全自动、领域无关的管道，用于从原始监管文档中提取结构化规则，无需人工标注、领域特定提示或标注数据，通过四阶段流程实现高质量规则提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监管文档包含LLM系统必须遵守的法律义务，但将密集、分层结构的法律文本转换为机器可读规则仍然是一个昂贵、专家密集型的过程，需要自动化解决方案。

Method: 四阶段管道：1) 源文档规范化为结构化Markdown；2) LLM驱动的语义分解为结构化规则单元；3) 多标准LLM-as-a-judge评估（19个维度）；4) 在有限再生预算内迭代修复低分提取，上游组件先于规则单元修复。

Result: 在金融、医疗和AI治理三个监管语料库上评估，De Jure在金融领域提取质量持续单调提升，三次迭代内达到峰值性能；在医疗和AI治理领域保持高性能；下游合规问答评估中，基于De Jure提取规则的RAG响应在73.8%-84.0%情况下优于先前工作。

Conclusion: 显式、可解释的评估标准可以替代复杂监管领域中的人工标注，为基于监管的LLM对齐提供可扩展且可审计的路径，提取保真度直接转化为下游效用。

Abstract: Regulatory documents encode legally binding obligations that LLM-based systems must respect. Yet converting dense, hierarchically structured legal text into machine-readable rules remains a costly, expert-intensive process. We present De Jure, a fully automated, domain-agnostic pipeline for extracting structured regulatory rules from raw documents, requiring no human annotation, domain-specific prompting, or annotated gold data. De Jure operates through four sequential stages: normalization of source documents into structured Markdown; LLM-driven semantic decomposition into structured rule units; multi-criteria LLM-as-a-judge evaluation across 19 dimensions spanning metadata, definitions, and rule semantics; and iterative repair of low-scoring extractions within a bounded regeneration budget, where upstream components are repaired before rule units are evaluated. We evaluate De Jure across four models on three regulatory corpora spanning finance, healthcare, and AI governance. On the finance domain, De Jure yields consistent and monotonic improvement in extraction quality, reaching peak performance within three judge-guided iterations. De Jure generalizes effectively to healthcare and AI governance, maintaining high performance across both open- and closed-source models. In a downstream compliance question-answering evaluation via RAG, responses grounded in De Jure extracted rules are preferred over prior work in 73.8% of cases at single-rule retrieval depth, rising to 84.0% under broader retrieval, confirming that extraction fidelity translates directly into downstream utility. These results demonstrate that explicit, interpretable evaluation criteria can substitute for human annotation in complex regulatory domains, offering a scalable and auditable path toward regulation-grounded LLM alignment.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [159] [Bench2Drive-VL: Benchmarks for Closed-Loop Autonomous Driving with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01259)
*Xiaosong Jia,Yuqian Shao,Zhenjie Yang,Qifeng Li,Zhiyuan Zhang,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Bench2Drive-VL扩展了Bench2Drive，为基于视觉语言模型的自动驾驶引入了首个闭环评估基准，通过自动生成多样化、基于行为的问答对，评估VLM在累积误差和分布外状态下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM4AD基准仅在开环环境下通过静态问答数据集评估场景理解能力，无法评估模型在累积误差和分布外输入下的性能。闭环评估在自动驾驶中已被广泛认为是更可靠的验证方法，但缺乏适用于VLM的闭环评估基准。

Method: 1) DriveCommenter：闭环生成器，自动为CARLA中所有驾驶情况生成多样化、基于行为的问答对，包括之前无法评估的严重偏离路线和越野情况。2) 统一协议和接口：允许现代VLM直接接入Bench2Drive闭环环境与传统智能体比较。3) 灵活推理和控制框架：支持多格式视觉输入和可配置的基于图的思维链执行。4) 完整的开发生态系统。

Result: 提出了Bench2Drive-VL，这是首个为基于视觉语言模型的自动驾驶设计的综合闭环基准。所有代码和标注数据集均已开源。

Conclusion: Bench2Drive-VL填补了VLM4AD领域缺乏闭环评估的空白，通过自动生成多样化、基于行为的问答对，能够全面评估VLM在累积误差和分布外状态下的性能，为VLM在自动驾驶中的可靠验证提供了重要工具。

Abstract: With the rise of vision-language models (VLM), their application for autonomous driving (VLM4AD) has gained significant attention. Meanwhile, in autonomous driving, closed-loop evaluation has become widely recognized as a more reliable validation method than open-loop evaluation, as it can evaluate the performance of the model under cumulative errors and out-of-distribution inputs. However, existing VLM4AD benchmarks evaluate the model`s scene understanding ability under open-loop, i.e., via static question-answer (QA) dataset. This kind of evaluation fails to assess the VLMs performance under out-of-distribution states rarely appeared in the human collected datasets.To this end, we present Bench2Drive-VL, an extension of Bench2Drive that brings closed-loop evaluation to VLM-based driving, which introduces: (1) DriveCommenter, a closed-loop generator that automatically generates diverse, behavior-grounded question-answer pairs for all driving situations in CARLA,including severe off-route and off-road deviations previously unassessable in simulation. (2) A unified protocol and interface that allows modern VLMs to be directly plugged into the Bench2Drive closed-loop environment to compare with traditional agents. (3) A flexible reasoning and control framework, supporting multi-format visual inputs and configurable graph-based chain-of-thought execution. (4) A complete development ecosystem. Together, these components form a comprehensive closed-loop benchmark for VLM4AD. All codes and annotated datasets are open sourced.

</details>


### [160] [Open-loop POMDP Simplification and Safe Skipping of Replanning with Formal Performance Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01352)
*Da Kong,Vadim Indelman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种具有形式性能保证的自适应开环简化框架，通过拓扑信念树自适应交织开环和闭环规划，显著降低POMDP规划复杂度，并提供可计算边界保证最优动作识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: POMDP为不确定性下的决策提供了原则性数学框架，但精确求解计算不可行。现有方法在计算复杂性和性能保证之间存在权衡，需要既能降低计算复杂度又能提供形式性能保证的解决方案。

Method: 提出自适应开环简化框架，通过拓扑信念树自适应交织开环和闭环规划。推导可高效计算的边界，为简化提供形式性能保证。开发安全跳过重规划的新框架，支持多步开环动作序列的理论保证。实现基于采样的求解器和随时求解器。

Result: 实证结果显示计算速度显著提升，同时保持可证明的性能保证。框架能够识别原始POMDP问题的即时最优动作，并在执行期间安全跳过重规划，推进了POMDP规划的可处理性和效率。

Conclusion: 该框架通过自适应开环简化和形式性能保证，有效解决了POMDP计算不可行性问题，在保持理论保证的同时显著提升计算效率，为POMDP规划提供了新的实用解决方案。

Abstract: Partially Observable Markov Decision Processes (POMDPs) provide a principled mathematical framework for decision-making under uncertainty. However, the exact solution to POMDPs is computationally intractable. In this paper, we address the computational intractability by introducing a novel framework for adaptive open-loop simplification with formal performance guarantees. Our method adaptively interleaves open-loop and closed-loop planning via a topology-based belief tree, enabling a significant reduction in planning complexity. The key contribution lies in the derivation of efficiently computable bounds which provide formal guarantees and can be used to ensure that our simplification can identify the immediate optimal action of the original POMDP problem. Our framework therefore provides computationally tractable performance guarantees for macro-actions within POMDPs. Furthermore, we propose a novel framework for safely skipping replanning during execution, supported by theoretical guarantees on multi-step open-loop action sequences. To the best of our knowledge, this framework is the first to address skipping replanning with formal performance guarantees. Practical online solvers for our proposed simplification are developed, including a sampling-based solver and an anytime solver. Empirical results demonstrate substantial computational speedups while maintaining provable performance guarantees, advancing the tractability and efficiency of POMDP planning.

</details>


### [161] [A soft and lightweight fabric-based pneumatic interface for multimodal fingertip tactile feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01390)
*Rui Chen,Daniele Leonardis,Antonio Frisoli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 基于织物的气动指尖触觉设备，通过热封工艺制造，重量仅2.1克，支持多种触觉模式，分类准确率超过90%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可穿戴指尖触觉设备难以同时实现足够的触觉输出、低质量、简单制造和无束缚便携性，需要一种新的技术方案来解决这一差距

Method: 采用织物基气动驱动技术，使用热塑性聚氨酯涂层织物通过数控热封工艺制造四个气动腔室，形成柔软、贴合的手指接口，配合腕戴式控制单元实现无束缚操作

Result: 机械和动态特性表征确认织物驱动器能产生足够的力、位移和带宽用于指尖触觉渲染；15名参与者的心理物理研究表明，在三种触觉模式（接触配置、定向滑动、振动触觉频率）上的分类准确率超过90%

Conclusion: 织物基气动驱动技术为轻量化、低成本、多模态指尖触觉接口提供了一条可行的技术路线

Abstract: Wearable fingertip haptic devices are critical for realistic interaction in virtual reality, augmented reality, and teleoperation, yet existing approaches struggle to simultaneously achieve adequate tactile output, low mass, simple fabrication, and untethered portability. Here we show that fabric-based pneumatic actuation can address this gap. Our device comprises four pneumatic chambers fabricated from thermoplastic polyurethane-coated fabric via computer numerical control heat-sealing, yielding a soft, conformable interface weighing 2.1 g that operates untethered with a wrist-mounted control unit. Mechanical and dynamic characterization confirms that the fabric actuators produce sufficient force, displacement, and bandwidth for fingertip tactile rendering. A psychophysical study with 15 participants demonstrates classification accuracy exceeding 90% across three distinct tactile modes -- contact configuration, directional sliding, and vibrotactile frequency. These findings establish fabric-based pneumatic actuation as a viable technology route for lightweight, low-cost, and multimodal fingertip haptic interfaces.

</details>


### [162] [Learning When to See and When to Feel: Adaptive Vision-Torque Fusion for Contact-Aware Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01414)
*Jiuzhou Lei,Chang Liu,Yu She,Xiao Liang,Minghui Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究比较了不同力/扭矩-视觉融合策略在基于扩散的机器人操作策略中的效果，并提出了一种自适应融合方法，在非接触阶段忽略力/扭矩信号，在接触阶段自适应利用视觉和扭矩信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯视觉策略在接触丰富和力敏感任务中表现不足，因为力/扭矩信号提供了接触动力学、对齐和交互质量的关键信息。虽然已有多种融合视觉和力/扭矩信号的方法，但缺乏对这些方法的系统比较。

Method: 1. 比较了不同的力/扭矩-视觉融合策略，包括辅助预测目标、专家混合架构和接触感知门控机制；2. 提出了一种自适应融合策略，在非接触阶段忽略力/扭矩信号，在接触阶段自适应利用视觉和扭矩信息；3. 在基于扩散的机器人操作策略框架下进行实验。

Result: 实验结果表明，提出的自适应融合方法在成功率上比最强基线提高了14%，证明了接触感知多模态融合对机器人操作的重要性。

Conclusion: 自适应力/扭矩-视觉融合策略能有效提升接触丰富任务的性能，强调了根据接触状态动态调整多模态信息融合的重要性。

Abstract: Vision-based policies have achieved a good performance in robotic manipulation due to the accessibility and richness of visual observations. However, purely visual sensing becomes insufficient in contact-rich and force-sensitive tasks where force/torque (F/T) signals provide critical information about contact dynamics, alignment, and interaction quality. Although various strategies have been proposed to integrate vision and F/T signals, including auxiliary prediction objectives, mixture-of-experts architectures, and contact-aware gating mechanisms, a comparison of these approaches remains lacking. In this work, we provide a comparison study of different F/T-vision integration strategies within diffusion-based manipulation policies. In addition, we propose an adaptive integration strategy that ignores F/T signals during non-contact phases while adaptively leveraging both vision and torque information during contact. Experimental results demonstrate that our method outperforms the strongest baseline by 14% in success rate, highlighting the importance of contact-aware multimodal fusion for robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [163] [Low-Burden LLM-Based Preference Learning: Personalizing Assistive Robots from Natural Language Feedback for Users with Paralysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01463)
*Keshav Shankar,Dan Ding,Wei Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个低负担的离线框架，将非结构化自然语言反馈直接转换为确定性机器人控制策略，用于物理辅助机器人个性化行为学习


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统偏好学习方法（如详尽成对比较）对严重运动障碍用户造成严重的身体和认知疲劳，需要更人性化的个性化行为学习方法

Method: 使用基于职业治疗实践框架（OTPF）的大型语言模型，将模糊的人类语音解码为明确的生理和心理需求，然后映射到透明决策树，并通过"LLM-as-a-Judge"自动验证代码结构安全性

Result: 在10名瘫痪成人的模拟备餐研究中验证，自然语言方法显著降低用户工作负荷，独立临床专家确认生成策略安全且准确反映用户偏好

Conclusion: 提出的框架成功将自然语言反馈转化为安全、个性化的机器人控制策略，为严重运动障碍用户提供了低负担的个性化辅助机器人行为学习方法

Abstract: Physically Assistive Robots (PARs) require personalized behaviors to ensure user safety and comfort. However, traditional preference learning methods, like exhaustive pairwise comparisons, cause severe physical and cognitive fatigue for users with profound motor impairments. To solve this, we propose a low-burden, offline framework that translates unstructured natural language feedback directly into deterministic robotic control policies. To safely bridge the gap between ambiguous human speech and robotic code, our pipeline uses Large Language Models (LLMs) grounded in the Occupational Therapy Practice Framework (OTPF). This clinical reasoning decodes subjective user reactions into explicit physical and psychological needs, which are then mapped into transparent decision trees. Before deployment, an automated "LLM-as-a-Judge" verifies the code's structural safety. We validated this system in a simulated meal preparation study with 10 adults with paralysis. Results show our natural language approach significantly reduces user workload compared to traditional baselines. Additionally, independent clinical experts confirmed the generated policies are safe and accurately reflect user preferences.

</details>


### [164] [Efficient Equivariant Transformer for Self-Driving Agent Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01466)
*Scott Xu,Dian Chen,Kelvin Wong,Chris Zhang,Kion Fallah,Raquel Urtasun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DriveGATr：一种基于几何代数的SE(2)-等变Transformer架构，用于自动驾驶中的智能体建模，无需显式成对相对位置编码，显著降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶中准确建模智能体行为至关重要，现有方法使用显式成对相对位置编码实现SE(2)-等变性，但计算成本随智能体数量呈二次方增长，限制了在大规模场景和批处理中的可扩展性。

Method: 提出DriveGATr架构：1) 将场景元素编码为2D投影几何代数ℝ*_{2,0,1}中的多向量；2) 使用等变Transformer块堆栈处理；3) 关键创新：通过多向量间的标准注意力建模几何关系，无需显式成对相对位置编码。

Result: 在Waymo Open Motion Dataset上的实验表明：1) 与最先进的交通模拟方法性能相当；2) 在性能与计算成本之间建立了更优的帕累托前沿；3) 显著降低了计算复杂度。

Conclusion: DriveGATr通过几何代数表示和标准注意力机制，实现了SE(2)-等变性而无需昂贵的显式成对相对位置编码，为自动驾驶智能体建模提供了高效且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurately modeling agent behaviors is an important task in self-driving. It is also a task with many symmetries, such as equivariance to the order of agents and objects in the scene or equivariance to arbitrary roto-translations of the entire scene as a whole; i.e., SE(2)-equivariance. The transformer architecture is a ubiquitous tool for modeling these symmetries. While standard self-attention is inherently permutation equivariant, explicit pairwise relative positional encodings have been the standard for introducing SE(2)-equivariance. However, this approach introduces an additional cost that is quadratic in the number of agents, limiting its scalability to larger scenes and batch sizes. In this work, we propose DriveGATr, a novel transformer-based architecture for agent modeling that achieves SE(2)-equivariance without the computational cost of existing methods. Inspired by recent advances in geometric deep learning, DriveGATr encodes scene elements as multivectors in the 2D projective geometric algebra $\mathbb{R}^*_{2,0,1}$ and processes them with a stack of equivariant transformer blocks. Crucially, DriveGATr models geometric relationships using standard attention between multivectors, eliminating the need for costly explicit pairwise relative positional encodings. Experiments on the Waymo Open Motion Dataset demonstrate that DriveGATr is comparable to the state-of-the-art in traffic simulation and establishes a superior Pareto front for performance vs computational cost.

</details>


### [165] [Distal-Stable Beam for Continuum Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01490)
*Ryouichi Saito,Takahiro Koide,Yuya Tanaka,Yasutaka Nakashima,Motoji Yamamoto,Ayato Kanada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种新型远端稳定梁结构，通过纯几何设计实现强刚度梯度，在保持中间段柔顺性的同时确保远端高刚性，为连续体机器人提供同时具备安全性和精度的新设计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体机器人适合受限环境但存在远端刚度低的问题，在外部载荷下会产生较大姿态误差。现有设计难以同时实现中间段的柔顺性和远端的高刚性。

Method: 提出远端稳定梁结构，由两根平行杆和一根收敛杆通过导向盘约束构成，引入几何耦合机制抑制变形模式并保持远端姿态。该设计通过纯几何方式实现刚度梯度，无需主动刚度调节。

Result: 实验显示远端刚度比中心高12倍，相比传统悬臂梁有约100倍的改进。该机制能在不进行主动刚度调节的情况下同时实现柔顺性和远端稳定性。

Conclusion: 远端稳定梁为需要同时具备安全性和精度的连续体机器人提供了新的设计方法，通过几何设计实现强刚度梯度，解决了传统连续体机器人远端刚度不足的问题。

Abstract: Continuum robots are well suited for constrained environments but suffer from low distal stiffness, resulting in large posture errors under external loads. In this paper, we propose a novel structural primitive, the Distal-Stable Beam, which achieves a strong stiffness gradient through purely geometric design, maintaining compliance in the intermediate section while ensuring high distal rigidity. The structure consists of two parallel rods and one convergent rod constrained by guide disks, introducing geometric coupling that suppresses deformation modes and preserves distal posture. Experiments show that the distal stiffness is 12 times higher than at the center, corresponding to an approximately 100-fold improvement over a conventional cantilever beam. The proposed mechanism enables simultaneous compliance and distal stability without active stiffness modulation, providing a new design approach for continuum robots requiring both safety and precision.

</details>


### [166] [Boosting Vision-Language-Action Finetuning with Feasible Action Neighborhood Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01570)
*Haochen Niu,Kanyu Zhang,Shuyu Yin,Qinghai Guo,Peilin Liu,Fei Wen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FAN引导的正则化方法，通过高斯先验使VLA模型输出分布与可行动作邻域几何对齐，提升机器人操作任务的样本效率和泛化能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实机器人操作中，每个状态存在可行动作邻域而非单一正确动作，但现有VLA训练方法直接继承语言设定，未利用FAN特性，导致泛化差和样本效率低

Method: 引入FAN引导的正则化器，通过高斯先验使模型输出分布与FAN几何对齐，促进围绕偏好方向和幅度的局部平滑单峰预测

Result: 在强化微调(RFT)和监督微调(SFT)实验中，方法显著提升样本效率、成功率，在分布内和分布外场景均表现优异

Conclusion: 通过与物理操作内在动作容差对齐，FAN引导正则化为样本高效、可泛化的VLA适应提供了原则性和实用方法

Abstract: In real-world robotic manipulation, states typically admit a neighborhood of near-equivalent actions. That is, for each state, there exist a feasible action neighborhood (FAN) rather than a single correct action, within which motions yield indistinguishable progress. However, prevalent VLA training methodologies are directly inherited from linguistic settings and do not exploit the FAN property, thus leading to poor generalization and low sample efficiency. To address this limitation, we introduce a FAN-guided regularizer that shapes the model's output distribution to align with the geometry of FAN. Concretely, we introduce a Gaussian prior that promotes locally smooth and unimodal predictions around the preferred direction and magnitude. In extensive experiments across both reinforced finetuning (RFT) and supervised finetuning (SFT), our method achieves significant improvement in sample efficiency, and success rate in both in-distribution and out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios. By aligning with the intrinsic action tolerance of physical manipulation, FAN-guided regularization provides a principled and practical method for sample-efficient, and generalizable VLA adaptation.

</details>


### [167] [Smooth Feedback Motion Planning with Reduced Curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01614)
*Aref Amiri,Steven M. LaValle*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种在单元分解上优化反馈运动规划的方法，通过对齐局部向量场和构建最大星形链来减少路径弯曲，降低控制能耗


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反馈运动规划算法在单元分解上生成的路径存在不必要的弯曲，导致运动速度慢、控制能耗高，需要更直接的轨迹生成方法

Method: 1) 引入启发式方法系统地对齐和分配局部向量场以产生更直接的轨迹；2) 提出新颖的几何算法构建目标周围的最大星形单纯形链，形成大"漏斗"区域；3) 在该区域内应用最优的直接到目标控制律

Result: 仿真显示方法显著减少路径弯曲（平均91.40%）和LQR控制能耗（平均45.47%），与采样和优化规划器相比具有时间效率和鲁棒性优势

Conclusion: 该方法在有限维单纯复形上有效，特别适用于低维（d≤3）构型空间，能显著改善反馈运动规划的直接性和效率

Abstract: Feedback motion planning over cell decompositions provides a robust method for generating collision-free robot motion with formal guarantees. However, existing algorithms often produce paths with unnecessary bending, leading to slower motion and higher control effort. This paper presents a computationally efficient method to mitigate this issue for a given simplicial decomposition. A heuristic is introduced that systematically aligns and assigns local vector fields to produce more direct trajectories, complemented by a novel geometric algorithm that constructs a maximal star-shaped chain of simplexes around the goal. This creates a large ``funnel'' in which an optimal, direct-to-goal control law can be safely applied. Simulations demonstrate that our method generates measurably more direct paths, reducing total bending by an average of 91.40\% and LQR control effort by an average of 45.47\%. Furthermore, comparative analysis against sampling-based and optimization-based planners confirms the time efficacy and robustness of our approach. While the proposed algorithms work over any finite-dimensional simplicial complex embedded in the collision-free subset of the configuration space, the practical application focuses on low-dimensional ($d\le3$) configuration spaces, where simplicial decomposition is computationally tractable.

</details>


### [168] [AURA: Multimodal Shared Autonomy for Real-World Urban Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01659)
*Yukai Ma,Honglin He,Selina Song,Wayne Wu,Bolei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AURA是一个辅助城市机器人自主性框架，通过将城市导航分解为高级人类指令和低级AI控制，减少人类认知负担，在类似接管条件下将接管频率降低44%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂城市环境中的长时程导航严重依赖持续人工操作，导致疲劳、效率降低和安全问题。现有共享自主性方法要求人类和AI在相同动作空间操作，造成高认知开销。

Method: 提出AURA多模态框架，将城市导航分解为高级人类指令和低级AI控制。包含空间感知指令编码器，将各种人类指令与视觉和空间上下文对齐。构建MM-CoS大规模数据集用于训练，包含遥操作和视觉语言描述。

Result: 仿真和真实世界实验表明，AURA能有效遵循人类指令，减少手动操作工作量，提高导航稳定性，并支持在线适应。在类似接管条件下，共享自主性框架将接管频率降低超过44%。

Conclusion: AURA通过分解导航任务为高级指令和低级控制，有效解决了共享自主性中的认知开销问题，显著减少了人类接管需求，提高了城市导航的效率和安全性。

Abstract: Long-horizon navigation in complex urban environments relies heavily on continuous human operation, which leads to fatigue, reduced efficiency, and safety concerns. Shared autonomy, where a Vision-Language AI agent and a human operator collaborate on maneuvering the mobile machine, presents a promising solution to address these issues. However, existing shared autonomy methods often require humans and AI to operate within the same action space, leading to high cognitive overhead. We present Assistive Urban Robot Autonomy (AURA), a new multi-modal framework that decomposes urban navigation into high-level human instruction and low-level AI control. AURA incorporates a Spatial-Aware Instruction Encoder to align various human instructions with visual and spatial context. To facilitate training, we construct MM-CoS, a large-scale dataset comprising teleoperation and vision-language descriptions. Experiments in simulation and the real world demonstrate that AURA effectively follows human instructions, reduces manual operation effort, and improves navigation stability, while enabling online adaptation. Moreover, under similar takeover conditions, our shared autonomy framework reduces the frequency of takeovers by more than 44%. Demo video and more detail are provided in the project page.

</details>


### [169] [3-D Relative Localization for Multi-Robot Systems with Angle and Self-Displacement Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01703)
*Chenyang Liang,Liangming Chen,Baoyi Cui,Jie Mei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于机器人间内角测量和自位移测量的三维相对定位框架，包括线性相对定位理论和处理测量噪声的MAP估计器


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在测量噪声存在下利用机器人间局部测量实现相对定位的挑战性问题

Method: 1. 提出线性相对定位理论，包括分布式线性相对定位算法和可定位性充分条件；2. 设计MAP估计器处理噪声，通过WTLS优化问题提供初始值避免局部最优；3. 结合神经密度估计器处理先验概率密度未知问题；4. 设计边缘化机制控制计算成本

Result: 机器人能够以纯线性方式确定邻居的相对位置和方向，MAP估计器通过WTLS提供更精确的初始值，结合NDE处理先验知识缺失，边缘化机制确保计算成本恒定

Conclusion: 该框架系统性地解决了三维相对定位问题，通过线性理论、MAP估计器优化、先验知识处理和计算成本控制，为噪声环境下的机器人相对定位提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: Realizing relative localization by leveraging inter-robot local measurements is a challenging problem, especially in the presence of measurement noise. Motivated by this challenge, in this paper we propose a novel and systematic 3-D relative localization framework based on inter-robot interior angle and self-displacement measurements. Initially, we propose a linear relative localization theory comprising a distributed linear relative localization algorithm and sufficient conditions for localizability. According to this theory, robots can determine their neighbors' relative positions and orientations in a purely linear manner. Subsequently, in order to deal with measurement noise, we present an advanced Maximum a Posterior (MAP) estimator by addressing three primary challenges existing in the MAP estimator. Firstly, it is common to formulate the MAP problem as an optimization problem, whose inherent non-convexity can result in local optima. To address this issue, we reformulate the linear computation process of the linear relative localization algorithm as a Weighted Total Least Squares (WTLS) optimization problem on manifolds. The optimal solution of the WTLS problem is more accurate, which can then be used as initial values when solving the optimization problem associated with the MAP problem, thereby reducing the risk of falling into local optima. The second challenge is the lack of knowledge of the prior probability density of the robots' relative positions and orientations at the initial time, which is required as an input for the MAP estimator. To deal with it, we combine the WTLS with a Neural Density Estimator (NDE). Thirdly, to prevent the increasing size of the relative positions and orientations to be estimated as the robots continuously move when solving the MAP problem, a marginalization mechanism is designed, which ensures that the computational cost remains constant.

</details>


### [170] [Hi-LOAM: Hierarchical Implicit Neural Fields for LiDAR Odometry and Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01720)
*Zhiliu Yang,Jianyuan Zhang,Lianhui Zhao,Jinyu Dai,Zhu Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Hi-LOAM：基于多尺度隐式神经表示的LiDAR里程计与建图框架，通过哈希表存储分层特征，采用无对应点云配准，实现自监督学习与优越泛化能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LOAM方法存在监督依赖或重建保真度不足的问题，难以准确描绘大规模复杂场景的细节特征，需要一种既能保持高精度又具备良好泛化能力的解决方案

Method: 1. 使用LiDAR点云作为输入，基于八叉树结构在多个层级的哈希表中学习和存储分层潜在特征；2. 通过浅层MLP将多尺度特征解码为有符号距离值进行建图；3. 采用无对应点云配准的scan-to-implicit匹配范式进行位姿估计；4. 整个训练过程完全自监督，无需预训练

Result: 在多个真实世界和合成数据集上的实验表明，Hi-LOAM在有效性和泛化能力方面均优于现有最先进方法，展现出卓越的性能表现

Conclusion: Hi-LOAM通过多尺度隐式神经表示和无对应点云配准技术，成功解决了现有LOAM方法的局限性，实现了高精度、自监督且具备强泛化能力的LiDAR里程计与建图系统

Abstract: LiDAR Odometry and Mapping (LOAM) is a pivotal technique for embodied-AI applications such as autonomous driving and robot navigation. Most existing LOAM frameworks are either contingent on the supervision signal, or lack of the reconstruction fidelity, which are deficient in depicting details of large-scale complex scenes. To overcome these limitations, we propose a multi-scale implicit neural localization and mapping framework using LiDAR sensor, called Hi-LOAM. Hi-LOAM receives LiDAR point cloud as the input data modality, learns and stores hierarchical latent features in multiple levels of hash tables based on an octree structure, then these multi-scale latent features are decoded into signed distance value through shallow Multilayer Perceptrons (MLPs) in the mapping procedure. For pose estimation procedure, we rely on a correspondence-free, scan-to-implicit matching paradigm to estimate optimal pose and register current scan into the submap. The entire training process is conducted in a self-supervised manner, which waives the model pre-training and manifests its generalizability when applied to diverse environments. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world and synthetic datasets demonstrate the superior performance, in terms of the effectiveness and generalization capabilities, of our Hi-LOAM compared to existing state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [171] [Causal Scene Narration with Runtime Safety Supervision for Vision-Language-Action Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01723)
*Yun Li,Yidu Zhang,Simon Thompson,Ehsan Javanmardi,Manabu Tsukada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CSN方法，通过意图-约束对齐、定量基础和结构化分离重构VLA文本输入，结合安全监督和偏好对齐训练，在CARLA评估中显著提升自动驾驶性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动驾驶的VLA模型需要处理多种文本输入（导航指令、危险警告、交通状态描述），但这些输入通常以不连贯的片段形式呈现，迫使模型自行发现哪些环境约束与当前操作相关，导致效率低下。

Method: 1. 引入因果场景叙述（CSN）：在推理时以零GPU成本重构VLA文本输入，包括意图-约束对齐、定量基础和结构化分离；2. 基于单纯形法的运行时安全监督；3. 使用带负对数似然正则化的Plackett-Luce DPO进行训练时对齐。

Result: 在CARLA多城镇闭环评估中，CSN使原始LMDrive的驾驶分数提升+31.1%，偏好对齐变体提升+24.5%。消融实验显示因果结构贡献了39.1%的性能增益，其余归因于信息内容。感知噪声消融证实CSN对现实感知误差具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CSN通过结构化文本输入显著提升VLA自动驾驶性能，因果结构是关键贡献因素。语义安全监督改善违规分数，而反应式碰撞时间监控会降低性能，表明VLA系统需要意图感知的监控方法。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models for autonomous driving must integrate diverse textual inputs, including navigation commands, hazard warnings, and traffic state descriptions, yet current systems often present these as disconnected fragments, forcing the model to discover on its own which environmental constraints are relevant to the current maneuver. We introduce Causal Scene Narration (CSN), which restructures VLA text inputs through intent-constraint alignment, quantitative grounding, and structured separation, at inference time with zero GPU cost. We complement CSN with Simplex-based runtime safety supervision and training-time alignment via Plackett-Luce DPO with negative log-likelihood (NLL) regularization. A multi-town closed-loop CARLA evaluation shows that CSN improves Driving Score by +31.1% on original LMDrive and +24.5% on the preference-aligned variant. A controlled ablation reveals that causal structure accounts for 39.1% of this gain, with the remainder attributable to information content alone. A perception noise ablation confirms that CSN's benefit is robust to realistic sensing errors. Semantic safety supervision improves Infraction Score, while reactive Time-To-Collision monitoring degrades performance, demonstrating that intent-aware monitoring is needed for VLA systems.

</details>


### [172] [Analysis of Efficient Transmission Methods of Grid Maps for Intelligent Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01753)
*Robin Dehler,Dominik Authaler,Aryan Thakur,Thomas Wodtko,Michael Buchholz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于补丁的通信管道，利用现有压缩算法高效传输网格地图数据，解决了智能交通系统中网格地图数据量过大影响分布式处理和V2X应用的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网格地图作为智能车辆环境建模的基础方法，虽然无需对物体类型或形状做出假设，但存在数据量过大的问题。补丁网格地图虽然通过调整单元格大小部分缓解了这一问题，但其数据量仍然过大，无法满足新型分布式处理架构和V2X应用的需求。

Method: 基于补丁网格地图方法，从通信角度研究尺寸问题，提出了一种补丁式通信管道，利用现有压缩算法高效传输网格地图数据。对车辆内部通信和V2X通信进行了全面分析，并通过两个真实世界实验设置验证了这些用例。

Result: 通过提出的补丁式通信管道和压缩算法，显著减少了网格地图数据的传输量，使其能够适应分布式处理和V2X应用的需求。实验验证了该管道在车辆内部通信和V2X通信中的有效性。

Conclusion: 研究总结了智能交通系统中高效传输网格地图数据的推荐指南，为实际应用提供了实用指导。基于补丁的通信管道结合压缩算法是解决网格地图数据量过大问题的有效方案。

Abstract: Grid mapping is a fundamental approach to modeling the environment of intelligent vehicles or robots. Compared with object-based environment modeling, grid maps offer the distinct advantage of representing the environment without requiring any assumptions about objects, such as type or shape. For grid-map-based approaches, the environment is divided into cells, each containing information about its respective area, such as occupancy. This representation of the entire environment is crucial for achieving higher levels of autonomy. However, it has the drawback that modeling the scene at the cell level results in inherently large data sizes. Patched grid maps tackle this issue to a certain extent by adapting cell sizes in specific areas. Nevertheless, the data sizes of patched grid maps are still too large for novel distributed processing setups or vehicle-to-everything (V2X) applications. Our work builds on a patch-based grid-map approach and investigates the size problem from a communication perspective. To address this, we propose a patch-based communication pipeline that leverages existing compression algorithms to transmit grid-map data efficiently. We provide a comprehensive analysis of this pipeline for both intra-vehicle and V2X-based communication. The analysis is verified for these use cases with two real-world experiment setups. Finally, we summarize recommended guidelines for the efficient transmission of grid-map data in intelligent transportation systems.

</details>


### [173] [Realistic Lip Motion Generation Based on 3D Dynamic Viseme and Coarticulation Modeling for Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01756)
*Sheng Li,Jingcheng Huang,Min Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于3D动态视位和协同发音建模的人形机器人唇部运动生成框架，通过构建中文发音的3D动态视位库，结合声韵母解耦和能量调制的协同发音机制，实现了高效准确的语音驱动唇部运动生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现逼真的唇部同步对于人形机器人自然的人机非语言交互至关重要。当前需要一种轻量级、高效且实用的语音驱动唇部运动生成方法，以提升人形机器人的交互自然度。

Method: 1. 基于中文发音理论分析，构建基于ARKit标准的3D动态视位库，提供连贯的唇部先验轨迹；2. 开发协同发音机制，通过声韵母解耦和能量调制解决连续语音流中的运动冲突；3. 设计策略将高维空间唇部运动重定向到人形头部平台的14自由度唇部驱动系统。

Result: 通过皮尔逊相关系数（PCC）和平均绝对加加速度（MAJ）指标的定量消融实验验证了所提架构的效率和准确性。该方法为语音驱动唇部运动生成提供了轻量级、高效且高度实用的范式。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种基于3D动态视位和协同发音建模的唇部运动生成框架，成功实现了人形机器人自然、高效的语音驱动唇部同步，为机器人非语言交互提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Realistic lip synchronization is essential for the natural human-robot non-verbal interaction of humanoid robots. Motivated by this need, this paper presents a lip motion generation framework based on 3D dynamic viseme and coarticulation modeling. By analyzing Chinese pronunciation theory, a 3D dynamic viseme library is constructed based on the ARKit standard, which offers coherent prior trajectories of lips. To resolve motion conflicts within continuous speech streams, a coarticulation mechanism is developed by incorporating initial-final (Shengmu-Yunmu) decoupling and energy modulation. After developing a strategy to retarget high-dimensional spatial lip motion to a 14-DOF lip actuation system of a humanoid head platform, the efficiency and accuracy of the proposed architecture is experimentally validated and demonstrated with quantitative ablation experiments using the metrics of the Pearson Correlation Coefficient (PCC) and the Mean Absolute Jerk (MAJ). This research offers a lightweight, efficient, and highly practical paradigm for the speech-driven lip motion generation of humanoid robots. The 3D dynamic viseme library and real-world deployment videos are available at {https://github.com/yuesheng21/Phoneme-to-Lip-14DOF}

</details>


### [174] [Preferential Bayesian Optimization with Crash Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01776)
*Johanna Menn,David Stenger,Sebastian Trimpe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CrashPBO：一种改进的偏好贝叶斯优化方法，允许用户在优化过程中报告实验崩溃，减少硬件系统（如无人机）的损坏风险


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人硬件系统（如四旋翼无人机）的参数优化中，标准偏好贝叶斯优化方法无法处理实验崩溃的情况，导致频繁的设备损坏、时间浪费和磨损

Method: 提出CrashPBO框架，扩展标准偏好贝叶斯优化，允许用户不仅提供偏好比较反馈，还能报告实验崩溃情况，从而避免探索会导致崩溃的参数

Result: 在合成函数基准测试中，该方法减少63%的崩溃次数并提高数据效率；在三个机器人平台上验证了方法的广泛适用性和可迁移性

Conclusion: CrashPBO提供了一个灵活、用户友好的框架，用于结合人类对偏好和崩溃的反馈进行参数学习，特别适用于硬件系统的安全优化

Abstract: Bayesian optimization is a popular black-box optimization method for parameter learning in control and robotics. It typically requires an objective function that reflects the user's optimization goal. However, in practical applications, this objective function is often inaccessible due to complex or unmeasurable performance metrics. Preferential Bayesian optimization (PBO) overcomes this limitation by leveraging human feedback through pairwise comparisons, eliminating the need for explicit performance quantification. When applying PBO to hardware systems, such as in quadcopter control, crashes can cause time-consuming experimental resets, wear and tear, or otherwise undesired outcomes. Standard PBO methods cannot incorporate feedback from such crashed experiments, resulting in the exploration of parameters that frequently lead to experimental crashes. We thus introduce CrashPBO, a user-friendly mechanism that enables users to both express preferences and report crashes during the optimization process. Benchmarking on synthetic functions shows that this mechanism reduces crashes by 63% and increases data efficiency. Through experiments on three robotics platforms, we demonstrate the wide applicability and transferability of CrashPBO, highlighting that it provides a flexible, user-friendly framework for parameter learning with human feedback on preferences and crashes.

</details>


### [175] [Posterior Optimization with Clipped Objective for Bridging Efficiency and Stability in Generative Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01860)
*Yuhui Chen,Haoran Li,Zhennan Jiang,Yuxing Qin,Yuxuan Wan,Weiheng Liu,Dongbin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: POCO是一个用于机器人操作的后验优化强化学习框架，通过期望最大化过程将奖励加权的隐式后验提炼到策略中，无需似然估计，采用离线到在线范式，可微调大型VLA模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然表达能力强的生成模型能够捕捉复杂多模态的动作分布，但通过RL对这些策略进行微调仍然面临不稳定和样本效率低的问题。

Method: POCO将策略改进形式化为时间动作块的后验推断问题，通过期望最大化过程将奖励加权的隐式后验提炼到策略中，无需似然估计。采用离线到在线范式，将在线探索锚定到预训练先验，模型无关设计可微调大型VLA模型。

Result: 在7个仿真基准和4个接触丰富的真实世界任务中，POCO防止了灾难性策略崩溃，优于最先进的基线方法，在真实世界任务中实现了96.7%的成功率。

Conclusion: POCO为微调表达性生成模型提供了一个稳定、样本高效的RL框架，能够有效提升机器人操作性能，特别是在接触丰富的真实世界任务中表现出色。

Abstract: Expressive generative models have advanced robotic manipulation by capturing complex, multi-modal action distributions over temporally extended trajectories. However, fine-tuning these policies via RL remains challenging due to instability and sample inefficiency. We introduce Posterior Optimization with Clipped Objective (POCO), a principled RL framework that formulates policy improvement as a posterior inference problem tailored for temporal action chunks. Through an Expectation-Maximization procedure, POCO distills a reward-weighted implicit posterior into the policy without likelihood estimation. Furthermore, POCO adopts an offline-to-online paradigm that anchors online exploration to pre-trained priors, and its model-agnostic design scales to fine-tune large VLA models without architectural modifications. Evaluations across 7 simulation benchmarks and 4 contact-rich real-world tasks demonstrate that POCO prevents catastrophic policy collapse, outperforms SOTA baselines, and achieves a 96.7% success rate on real-world tasks. Videos are available at our project website https://cccedric.github.io/poco/.

</details>


### [176] [Integrated Identification of Collaborative Robots for Robot Assisted 3D Printing Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01991)
*Alessandro Dimauro,Davide Tebaldi,Fabio Pini,Luigi Biagiotti,Francesco Leali*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于模型的集成参数辨识方法，用于机器人辅助增材制造系统，通过五步流程确保参数物理一致性，并在6自由度协作机器人上验证了模型精度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人辅助增材制造克服了传统笛卡尔系统的尺寸和运动学限制，但机器人动态复杂性增加带来了精度、控制和误差预测的挑战，需要可靠的动态模型

Method: 采用基于模型的集成参数辨识方法，包括五步流程：几何和惯性分析、摩擦参数辨识、控制器参数辨识、剩余参数辨识，确保参数物理一致性

Result: 在6自由度协作机器人的热塑性挤出工艺案例研究中验证，实验结果表明实际机器人与辨识模型之间匹配良好，证明了精度、控制和误差预测的潜在提升

Conclusion: 集成建模方法能够在协作机器人典型的传感和编程限制下获得可靠的动态模型，为机器人辅助3D打印过程的精度提升和控制优化提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: In recent years, the integration of additive manufacturing (AM) and industrial robotics has opened new perspectives for the production of complex components, particularly in the automotive sector. Robot-assisted additive manufacturing processes overcome the dimensional and kinematic limitations of traditional Cartesian systems, enabling non-planar deposition and greater geometric flexibility. However, the increasing dynamic complexity of robotic manipulators introduces challenges related to precision, control, and error prediction. This work proposes a model-based approach equipped with an integrated identification procedure of the system's parameters, including the robot, the actuators and the controllers. We show that the integrated modeling procedure allows to obtain a reliable dynamic model even in the presence of sensory and programming limitations typical of collaborative robots. The manipulator's dynamic model is identified through an integrated five step methodology: starting with geometric and inertial analysis, followed by friction and controller parameters identification, all the way to the remaining parameters identification. The proposed procedure intrinsically ensures the physical consistency of the identified parameters. The identification approach is validated on a real world case study involving a 6-Degrees-Of-Freedom (DoFs) collaborative robot used in a thermoplastic extrusion process. The very good matching between the experimental results given by actual robot and those given by the identified model shows the potential enhancement of precision, control, and error prediction in Robot Assisted 3D Printing Processes.

</details>


### [177] [Bridging Discrete Planning and Continuous Execution for Redundant Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02021)
*Teng Yan,Yue Yu,Yihan Liu,Bingzhuo Zhong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个连接离散规划与连续执行的桥梁框架，通过改进规划侧的动作设计和执行侧的逆运动学层，显著提升7自由度冗余机械臂在体素网格强化学习路径规划中的执行质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体素网格强化学习在冗余机械臂路径规划中广泛应用，但直接通过逐点数值逆运动学执行会导致步长抖动、关节突变和奇异配置附近的不稳定性问题。

Method: 1) 规划侧：引入步长归一化的26邻域笛卡尔动作和几何平局决胜机制；2) 执行侧：实现任务优先级阻尼最小二乘逆运动学层，将末端执行器位置作为主要任务，姿态和关节居中作为次要任务在零空间处理，结合信任区域裁剪和关节速度约束。

Result: 在7自由度机械臂的随机稀疏、中等和密集环境中，密集场景的规划成功率从约0.58提升至1.00，代表性路径长度从约1.53米缩短至1.10米，末端执行器误差保持在1毫米以下，峰值关节加速度降低一个数量级以上。

Conclusion: 该桥梁框架在不修改离散规划器本身的情况下，显著提高了基于体素的强化学习路径在冗余机械臂上的连续执行质量，解决了离散规划与连续执行之间的不匹配问题。

Abstract: Voxel-grid reinforcement learning is widely adopted for path planning in redundant manipulators due to its simplicity and reproducibility. However, direct execution through point-wise numerical inverse kinematics on 7-DoF arms often yields step-size jitter, abrupt joint transitions, and instability near singular configurations. This work proposes a bridging framework between discrete planning and continuous execution without modifying the discrete planner itself. On the planning side, step-normalized 26-neighbor Cartesian actions and a geometric tie-breaking mechanism are introduced to suppress unnecessary turns and eliminate step-size oscillations. On the execution side, a task-priority damped least-squares (TP-DLS) inverse kinematics layer is implemented. This layer treats end-effector position as a primary task, while posture and joint centering are handled as subordinate tasks projected into the null space, combined with trust-region clipping and joint velocity constraints. On a 7-DoF manipulator in random sparse, medium, and dense environments, this bridge raises planning success in dense scenes from about 0.58 to 1.00, shortens representative path length from roughly 1.53 m to 1.10 m, and while keeping end-effector error below 1 mm, reduces peak joint accelerations by over an order of magnitude, substantially improving the continuous execution quality of voxel-based RL paths on redundant manipulators.

</details>


### [178] [O-ConNet: Geometry-Aware End-to-End Inference of Over-Constrained Spatial Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02038)
*Haoyu Sun,Meng Zhao,Tianhao Wang,Jianxu Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: O-ConNet：一种端到端深度学习框架，仅需三个稀疏可达点即可推断空间过约束机构的结构参数并重建完整运动轨迹，无需在推理时显式求解约束方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在科学发现中展现出强大潜力，但其建模宏观刚体运动学约束的能力尚未充分探索。本研究旨在解决空间过约束机构的结构参数推断问题，特别是在极稀疏观测条件下的逆设计挑战。

Method: 提出O-ConNet端到端框架，通过仅三个稀疏可达点输入，直接推断机构的结构参数并重建完整运动轨迹。该方法避免了推理过程中显式求解约束方程，而是通过深度学习直接学习机构的几何约束关系。

Result: 在包含42,860个有效样本的自建Bennett 4R数据集上，O-ConNet取得Param-MAE 0.276±0.077和Traj-MAE 0.145±0.018（10次运行均值±标准差），分别比最强序列基线（LSTM-Seq2Seq）提升65.1%和88.2%。

Conclusion: 端到端学习能够有效捕捉闭环几何结构，为极稀疏观测条件下的空间过约束机构逆设计提供了实用途径。该方法展示了深度学习在建模复杂机械约束方面的潜力。

Abstract: Deep learning has shown strong potential for scientific discovery, but its ability to model macroscopic rigid-body kinematic constraints remains underexplored. We study this problem on spatial over-constrained mechanisms and propose O-ConNet, an end-to-end framework that infers mechanism structural parameters from only three sparse reachable points while reconstructing the full motion trajectory, without explicitly solving constraint equations during inference. On a self-constructed Bennett 4R dataset of 42,860 valid samples, O-ConNet achieves Param-MAE 0.276 +/- 0.077 and Traj-MAE 0.145 +/- 0.018 (mean +/- std over 10 runs), outperforming the strongest sequence baseline (LSTM-Seq2Seq) by 65.1 percent and 88.2 percent, respectively. These results suggest that end-to-end learning can capture closed-loop geometric structure and provide a practical route for inverse design of spatial over-constrained mechanisms under extremely sparse observations.

</details>


### [179] [HyVGGT-VO: Tightly Coupled Hybrid Dense Visual Odometry with Feed-Forward Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02107)
*Junxiang Pan,Lipu Zhou,Baojie Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HyVGGT-VO提出了一种结合稀疏VO计算效率和前馈模型密集重建能力的新型框架，通过自适应混合跟踪前端和分层优化，在保持实时性的同时显著提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在两难：前馈模型能实现密集重建但计算负担重，难以实时输出位姿；传统稀疏方法计算效率高、能高频输出位姿，但缺乏密集重建能力。需要结合两者优势。

Method: 1) 设计自适应混合跟踪前端，动态切换传统光流和VGGT跟踪头以确保鲁棒性；2) 提出分层优化框架，联合优化VO位姿和VGGT预测尺度以保证全局尺度一致性；3) 紧密耦合传统VO框架与VGGT前馈模型。

Result: 相比现有VGGT方法处理速度提升约5倍；在室内EuRoC数据集上平均轨迹误差降低85%，在室外KITTI基准上降低12%；实现了实时位姿估计与密集重建的平衡。

Conclusion: HyVGGT-VO成功结合了稀疏VO的高效性和前馈模型的密集重建能力，首次实现了传统VO框架与VGGT的紧密耦合，在保持实时性的同时显著提升了精度，为视觉里程计系统提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Dense visual odometry (VO), which provides pose estimation and dense 3D reconstruction, serves as the cornerstone for applications ranging from robotics to augmented reality. Recently, feed-forward models have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in dense mapping. However, when these models are used in dense visual SLAM systems, their heavy computational burden restricts them to yielding sparse pose outputs at keyframes while still failing to achieve real-time pose estimation. In contrast, traditional sparse methods provide high computational efficiency and high-frequency pose outputs, but lack the capability for dense reconstruction. To address these limitations, we propose HyVGGT-VO, a novel framework that combines the computational efficiency of sparse VO with the dense reconstruction capabilities of feed-forward models. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to tightly couple a traditional VO framework with VGGT, a state-of-the-art feed-forward model. Specifically, we design an adaptive hybrid tracking frontend that dynamically switches between traditional optical flow and the VGGT tracking head to ensure robustness. Furthermore, we introduce a hierarchical optimization framework that jointly refines VO poses and the scale of VGGT predictions to ensure global scale consistency. Our approach achieves an approximately 5x processing speedup compared to existing VGGT-based methods, while reducing the average trajectory error by 85% on the indoor EuRoC dataset and 12% on the outdoor KITTI benchmark. Our code will be publicly available upon acceptance. Project page: https://geneta2580.github.io/HyVGGT-VO.io.

</details>


### [180] [Cross-Modal Visuo-Tactile Object Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02108)
*Anirvan Dutta,Simone Tasciotti,Claudia Cusseddu,Ang Li,Panayiota Poirazi,Julijana Gjorgjieva,Etienne Burdet,Patrick van der Smagt,Mohsen Kaboli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出跨模态潜在滤波器(CMLF)，通过贝叶斯推理学习物理对象属性的结构化因果潜在状态空间，实现视觉与触觉的双向先验传递，提升机器人多感官感知的鲁棒性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人操作中，物理属性估计对安全高效交互至关重要。视觉和触觉提供互补信息，但这些属性间接可观测且难以精确建模，现有方法主要关注强力传感器融合或静态跨模态对齐，缺乏对不确定性和信念随时间演化的考虑。

Method: 受人类多感官感知和主动推理启发，提出跨模态潜在滤波器(CMLF)，学习物理对象属性的结构化因果潜在状态空间。支持视觉与触觉间的双向跨模态先验传递，通过随时间演化的贝叶斯推理过程整合感官证据。

Result: 真实机器人实验表明，CMLF在不确定性条件下比基线方法提高了潜在物理属性估计的效率和鲁棒性。模型还表现出类似人类的感知耦合现象，包括对跨模态错觉的易感性以及学习跨感官关联的相似轨迹。

Conclusion: CMLF为机器人多感官感知的可泛化、鲁棒且物理一致的跨模态集成迈出了重要一步，实现了类似人类的感知耦合现象，为复杂物理交互提供了更自然的感知框架。

Abstract: Estimating physical properties is critical for safe and efficient autonomous robotic manipulation, particularly during contact-rich interactions. In such settings, vision and tactile sensing provide complementary information about object geometry, pose, inertia, stiffness, and contact dynamics, such as stick-slip behavior. However, these properties are only indirectly observable and cannot always be modeled precisely (e.g., deformation in non-rigid objects coupled with nonlinear contact friction), making the estimation problem inherently complex and requiring sustained exploitation of visuo-tactile sensory information during action. Existing visuo-tactile perception frameworks have primarily emphasized forceful sensor fusion or static cross-modal alignment, with limited consideration of how uncertainty and beliefs about object properties evolve over time. Inspired by human multi-sensory perception and active inference, we propose the Cross-Modal Latent Filter (CMLF) to learn a structured, causal latent state-space of physical object properties. CMLF supports bidirectional transfer of cross-modal priors between vision and touch and integrates sensory evidence through a Bayesian inference process that evolves over time. Real-world robotic experiments demonstrate that CMLF improves the efficiency and robustness of latent physical properties estimation under uncertainty compared to baseline approaches. Beyond performance gains, the model exhibits perceptual coupling phenomena analogous to those observed in humans, including susceptibility to cross-modal illusions and similar trajectories in learning cross-sensory associations. Together, these results constitutes a significant step toward generalizable, robust and physically consistent cross-modal integration for robotic multi-sensory perception.

</details>


### [181] [ROS 2-Based LiDAR Perception Framework for Mobile Robots in Dynamic Production Environments, Utilizing Synthetic Data Generation, Transformation-Equivariant 3D Detection and Multi-Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02109)
*Lukas Bergs,Tan Chung,Marmik Thakkar,Alexander Moriz,Amon Göppert,Chinnawut Nantabut,Robert Schmitt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于ROS的LiDAR框架，集成合成数据训练的变换等变3D检测与基于中心姿态的多目标跟踪，提升工业移动机械臂在动态环境中的感知鲁棒性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态生产环境中自适应机器人需要鲁棒的感知能力，包括6D姿态估计和多目标跟踪。现有方法存在对真实数据依赖、噪声鲁棒性不足以及时空一致性等问题

Method: 基于ROS的LiDAR框架，集成合成数据训练的变换等变3D检测方法，结合基于中心姿态的多目标跟踪技术，减少对真实数据的依赖并提升时空一致性

Result: 在72个场景中通过运动捕捉技术验证，单独姿态估计的IoU为62.6%，集成多目标跟踪后提升至83.12%。LiDAR框架的HOTA达到91.12%，显著提升工业移动机械臂感知系统的鲁棒性和通用性

Conclusion: 提出的LiDAR框架通过合成数据训练和多目标跟踪集成，有效解决了动态工业环境中感知系统的鲁棒性问题，为工业移动机械臂提供了更可靠的感知解决方案

Abstract: Adaptive robots in dynamic production environments require robust perception capabilities, including 6D pose estimation and multi-object tracking. To address limitations in real-world data dependency, noise robustness, and spatiotemporal consistency, a LiDAR framework based on the Robot Operating System integrating a synthetic-data-trained Transformation-Equivariant 3D Detection with multi-object-tracking leveraging center poses is proposed. Validated across 72 scenarios with motion capture technology, overall results yield an Intersection over Union of 62.6% for standalone pose estimation, rising to 83.12% with multi-object-tracking integration. Our LiDAR-based framework achieves 91.12% of Higher Order Tracking Accuracy, advancing robustness and versatility of LiDAR-based perception systems for industrial mobile manipulators.

</details>


### [182] [PRO-SPECT: Probabilistically Safe Scalable Planning for Energy-Aware Coordinated UAV-UGV Teams in Stochastic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02142)
*Roger Fowler,Cahit Ikbal Er,Benjamin Johnsenberg,Yasin Yazicioglu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PRO-SPECT算法，为UAV-UGV团队在随机环境中进行风险约束的能源感知路径规划，确保能量耗尽概率不超过用户指定风险水平


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常假设确定性旅行时间或使用固定鲁棒性边界，无法有效处理随机环境中的不确定性。需要一种能够建模旅行时间随机性并保证整个任务失败概率不超过指定风险水平的方法

Method: 将问题建模为混合整数规划，提出PRO-SPECT多项式时间算法，支持离线规划和在线重规划，在扰动发生时保持风险边界

Result: 提供解决方案可行性和时间复杂度的理论结果，通过数值比较和仿真验证方法性能

Conclusion: PRO-SPECT算法能够为UAV-UGV团队在随机环境中生成风险约束的能源感知计划，在保证理论风险边界的同时支持动态适应

Abstract: We consider energy-aware planning for an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) and unmanned ground vehicle (UGV) team operating in a stochastic environment. The UAV must visit a set of air points in minimum time while respecting energy constraints, relying on the UGV as a mobile charging station. Unlike prior work that assumed deterministic travel times or used fixed robustness margins, we model travel times as random variables and bound the probability of failure (energy depletion) across the entire mission to a user-specified risk level. We formulate the problem as a Mixed-Integer Program and propose PRO-SPECT, a polynomial-time algorithm that generates risk-bounded plans. The algorithm supports both offline planning and online re-planning, enabling the team to adapt to disturbances while preserving the risk bound. We provide theoretical results on solution feasibility and time complexity. We also demonstrate the performance of our method via numerical comparisons and simulations.

</details>


### [183] [A virtual-variable-length method for robust inverse kinematics of multi-segment continuum robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02256)
*Weiting Feng,Federico Renda,Yunjie Yang,Francesco Giorgio-Serchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出虚拟变长(VVL)方法解决多段连续体机械臂逆运动学问题，相比传统雅可比方法提升20%收敛成功率，减少40-80%平均迭代次数


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统雅可比逆运动学求解器在从中性/静止配置初始化时，通常表现出收敛速度慢，在某些条件下甚至无法收敛（死锁）。需要一种更鲁棒的方法来解决多段连续体机械臂的逆运动学问题。

Method: 提出虚拟变长(VVL)方法，在求解迭代过程中引入虚构的段长度变化，赋予虚拟轴向自由度，从而缓解不利行为和约束，实现或加速收敛。

Result: 在超过180万次随机试验中（涵盖2到7段机械臂），VVL方法相比基准雅可比和阻尼最小二乘求解器，收敛成功率提升高达20%，在相同精度阈值(10^-4-10^-8)下平均迭代次数减少40-80%。

Conclusion: VVL方法能有效解决多段连续体机械臂逆运动学求解中的收敛问题，特别是对边界邻近配置导致的死锁情况有显著改善，提供了一种更鲁棒的求解方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes a new, robust method to solve the inverse kinematics (IK) of multi-segment continuum manipulators. Conventional Jacobian-based solvers, especially when initialized from neutral/rest configurations, often exhibit slow convergence and, in certain conditions, may fail to converge (deadlock). The Virtual-Variable-Length (VVL) method proposed here introduces fictitious variations of segments' length during the solution iteration, conferring virtual axial degrees of freedom that alleviate adverse behaviors and constraints, thus enabling or accelerating convergence. Comprehensive numerical experiments were conducted to compare the VVL method against benchmark Jacobian-based and Damped Least Square IK solvers. Across more than $1.8\times 10^6$ randomized trials covering manipulators with two to seven segments, the proposed approach achieved up to a 20$\%$ increase in convergence success rate over the benchmark and a 40-80$\%$ reduction in average iteration count under equivalent accuracy thresholds ($10^{-4}-10^{-8}$). While deadlocks are not restricted to workspace boundaries and may occur at arbitrary poses, our empirical study identifies boundary-proximal configurations as a frequent cause of failed convergence and the VVL method mitigates such occurrences over a statistical sample of test cases.

</details>
